<h2 id="inbound-ip-addresses" class="group">Inbound IP addresses<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_8emaav5ul5u9fmbtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>These are the IP addresses where Anthropic services receive incoming connections.</p>
<h3 id="i-pv4" class="group">IPv4<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_8umaav5ul5u9fmbtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p><code>160.79.104.0/23</code></p>
<h3 id="i-pv6" class="group">IPv6<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_9emaav5ul5u9fmbtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>

<h2 id="outbound-ip-addresses" class="group">Outbound IP addresses<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_9umaav5ul5u9fmbtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>These are the stable IP addresses that Anthropic uses for outbound requests (for example, when making MCP tool calls to external servers).</p>
<h3 id="i-pv4-2" class="group">IPv4<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_aemaav5ul5u9fmbtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p><code>160.79.104.0/21</code></p>
<h3 id="phased-out-ip-addresses" class="group">Phased out IP addresses<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_aumaav5ul5u9fmbtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p>The following IP addresses are no longer in use by Anthropic. If you have previously allowlisted these addresses, you should remove them from your firewall rules.</p>
